description: 'カラーパレット、フォント、カードの影やアニメーションの設定。',\n    status: 'done',\n    priority: 'medium',\n    dueDate: '2025-04-02',\n    createdAt: '2025-03-31',\n  },\n];\n\nconst columnConfigs: { status: TaskStatus; title: string }[] = [\n  { status: 'todo', title: '未着手' },\n  { status: 'in_progress', title: '作業中' },\n  { status: 'done', title: '完了' },\n];\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<TaskItem[]>(initialTasks);\n  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');\n  const [priorityFilter, setPriorityFilter] = useState<string>('all');\n  const [draggedTaskId, setDraggedTaskId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // モーダル管理\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [taskToEdit, setTaskToEdit] = useState<TaskItem | null>(null);\n  const [modalInitialStatus, setModalInitialStatus] = useState<TaskStatus>('todo');\n\n  // ドラッグ処理\n  const handleDragStart = (_e: DragEvent, id: string) => {\n    setDraggedTaskId(id);\n  };\n\n  const handleDragOver = (e: DragEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n  };\n\n  const handleDragLeave = (_e: DragEvent) => {\n    // プレビュー内でのちらつき防止\n  };\n\n  const handleDrop = (e: DragEvent, targetStatus: TaskStatus) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!draggedTaskId) return;\n\n    setTasks((prevTasks) =>\n      prevTasks.map((task) =>\n        task.id === draggedTaskId ? { ...task, status: targetStatus } : task\n      )\n    );\n    setDraggedTaskId(null);\n  };\n\n  // タスク操作ロジック\n  const handleMoveTask = (id: string, newStatus: TaskStatus) => {\n    setTasks((prevTasks) =>\n      prevTasks.map((task) =>\n        task.id === id ? { ...task, status: newStatus } : task\n      )\n    );\n  };\n\n  const handleDeleteTask = (id: string) => {\n    setTasks((prevTasks) => prevTasks.filter((task) => task.id !== id));\n  };\n\n  const handleOpenAddModal = (status: TaskStatus = 'todo') => {\n    setTaskToEdit(null);\n    setModalInitialStatus(status);\n    setIsModalOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const handleOpenEditModal = (task: TaskItem) => {\n    setTaskToEdit(task);\n    setModalInitialStatus(task.status);\n    setIsModalOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const handleCloseModal = () => {\n    setIsModalOpen(false);\n    setTaskToEdit(null);\n  };\n\n  const handleSaveTask = (\n    taskData: Omit<TaskItem, 'id' | 'createdAt'>,\n    editId?: string\n  ) => {\n    if (editId) {\n      setTasks((prevTasks) =>\n        prevTasks.map((task) =>\n          task.id === editId ? { ...task, ...taskData } : task\n        )\n      );\n    } else {\n      const newTask: TaskItem = {\n        ...taskData,\n        id: `task-${Date.now()}`,\n        createdAt: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],\n      };\n      setTasks((prevTasks) => [newTask, ...prevTasks]);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // フィルタリング\n  const filteredTasks = tasks.filter((task) => {\n    const matchesSearch =\n      task.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||\n      task.description.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());\n    const matchesPriority =\n      priorityFilter === 'all' || task.priority === priorityFilter;\n\n    return matchesSearch && matchesPriority;\n  });\n\n  const totalCount = tasks.length;\n  const inProgressCount = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'in_progress').length;\n  const doneCount = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'done').length;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"kanban-app\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"header-title\">\n          <ListTodo size={28} color=\"#3b82f6\" />\n          カンバンボード\n        </h1>\n        <div className=\"header-stats\">\n          <span className=\"stat-badge\">\n            全タスク: <strong>{totalCount}</strong> 件\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"stat-badge\">\n            <Clock size={14} color=\"#d97706\" />\n            作業中: <strong>{inProgressCount}</strong>\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"stat-badge\">\n            <CheckCircle2 size={14} color=\"#16a34a\" />\n            完了: <strong>{doneCount}</strong>\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className=\"app-toolbar\">\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', flex: 1, minWidth: '220px' }}>\n          <Search size={18} color=\"#64748b\" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            className=\"search-input\"\n            placeholder=\"タスクを検索...\"\n            value={searchQuery}\n            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        <select\n          className=\"priority-filter-select\"\n          value={priorityFilter}\n          onChange={(e) => setPriorityFilter(e.target.value)}\n        >\n          <option value=\"all\">すべての優先度</option>\n          <option value=\"high\">優先度: 高</option>\n          <option value=\"medium\">優先度: 中</option>\n          <option value=\"low\">優先度: 低</option>\n        </select>\n\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"add-task-btn\"\n          onClick={() => handleOpenAddModal('todo')}\n        >\n          <Plus size={18} />\n          新規タスク\n        </button>\n      </section>\n\n      <main className=\"kanban-board\">\n        {columnConfigs.map((col) => {\n          const colTasks = filteredTasks.filter((t) => t.status === col.status);\n          return (\n            <KanbanColumn\n              key={col.status}\n              status={col.status}\n              title={col.title}\n              tasks={colTasks}\n              onDragStart={handleDragStart}\n              onDragOver={handleDragOver}\n              onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}\n              onDrop={handleDrop}\n              onOpenAddModal={handleOpenAddModal}\n              onOpenEditModal={handleOpenEditModal}\n              onDeleteTask={handleDeleteTask}\n              onMoveTask={handleMoveTask}\n            />\n          );\n        })}\n      </main>\n\n      <TaskModal\n        isOpen={isModalOpen}\n        taskToEdit={taskToEdit}\n        initialStatus={modalInitialStatus}\n        onClose={handleCloseModal}\n        onSave={handleSaveTask}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/TaskCard.tsx","content":"import { useState, type DragEvent } from 'react';\nimport { Calendar, Edit3, Trash2, ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';\nimport type { TaskCardProps, TaskPriority } from '../types';\n\nconst priorityLabels: Record<TaskPriority, string> = {\n  high: '高',\n  medium: '中',\n  low: '低',\n};\n\nconst priorityClasses: Record<TaskPriority, string> = {\n  high: 'card-priority-high',\n  medium: 'card-priority-medium',\n  low: 'card-priority-low',\n};\n\nconst getLocalDateString = (): string => {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const year = now.getFullYear();\n  const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');\n  const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');\n  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;\n};\n\nconst TaskCard = ({\n  task,\n  onDragStart,\n  onOpenEditModal,\n  onDeleteTask,\n  onMoveTask,\n}: TaskCardProps) => {\n  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  const handleDragStart = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    setIsDragging(true);\n    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', task.id);\n    onDragStart(e, task.id);\n  };\n\n  const handleDragEnd = () => {\n    setIsDragging(false);\n  };\n\n  const handlePreviousStatus = () => {\n    if (task.status === 'done') {\n      onMoveTask(task.id, 'in_progress');\n    } else if (task.status === 'in_progress') {\n      onMoveTask(task.id, 'todo');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleNextStatus = () => {\n    if (task.status === 'todo') {\n      onMoveTask(task.id, 'in_progress');\n    } else if (task.status === 'in_progress') {\n      onMoveTask(task.id, 'done');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const todayStr = getLocalDateString();\n  const isOverdue = task.status !== 'done' && Boolean(task.dueDate) && task.dueDate < todayStr;\n  const cardClassName = `task-card ${isDragging ? 'task-card-dragging' : ''}`;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={cardClassName}\n      draggable\n      onDragStart={handleDragStart}\n      onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}\n    >\n      <div className=\"card-header\">\n        <span className={`card-priority ${priorityClasses[task.priority]}`}>\n          {priorityLabels[task.priority]}\n        </span>\n        <div className=\"card-actions\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"card-icon-btn\"\n            title=\"編集\"\n            onClick={() => onOpenEditModal(task)}\n          >\n            <Edit3 size={15} />\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"card-icon-btn btn-danger\"\n            title=\"削除\"\n            onClick={() => onDeleteTask(task.id)}\n          >\n            <Trash2 size={15} />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <h4 className=\"card-title\">{task.title}</h4>\n\n      {task.description && (\n        <p className=\"card-desc\">{task.description}</p>\n      )}\n\n      <div className=\"card-footer\">\n        <div\n          className=\"card-due-date\"\n          style={isOverdue ? { color: 'var(--color-danger)', fontWeight: 600 } : undefined}\n          title={isOverdue ? '期限が過ぎています' : undefined}\n        >\n          {isOverdue ? <AlertCircle size={13} /> : <Calendar size={13} />}\n          <span>{task.dueDate ? (isOverdue ? `${task.dueDate} (期限切)` : task.dueDate) : '期日なし'}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"card-actions\">\n          {task.status !== 'todo' && (\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className=\"move-btn\"\n              title=\"前のステータスへ移動\"\n              onClick={handlePreviousStatus}\n            >\n              <ArrowLeft size={13} />\n            </button>\n          )}\n          {task.status !== 'done' && (\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className=\"move-btn\"\n              title=\"次のステータスへ移動\"\n              onClick={handleNextStatus}\n            >\n              <ArrowRight size={13} />\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default TaskCard;","language":"tsx"}

TaskModalProps, TaskPriority, TaskStatus } from '../types';\n\nconst getLocalDateString = (): string => {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const year = now.getFullYear();\n  const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');\n  const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');\n  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;\n};\n\nconst TaskModal = ({\n  isOpen,\n  taskToEdit,\n  initialStatus,\n  onClose,\n  onSave,\n}: TaskModalProps) => {\n  const [title, setTitle] = useState<string>('');\n  const [description, setDescription] = useState<string>('');\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<TaskStatus>(initialStatus);\n  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<TaskPriority>('medium');\n  const [dueDate, setDueDate] = useState<string>('');\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (taskToEdit) {\n      setTitle(taskToEdit.title);\n      setDescription(taskToEdit.description);\n      setStatus(taskToEdit.status);\n      setPriority(taskToEdit.priority);\n      setDueDate(taskToEdit.dueDate);\n    } else {\n      setTitle('');\n      setDescription('');\n      setStatus(initialStatus);\n      setPriority('medium');\n      setDueDate(getLocalDateString());\n    }\n  }, [taskToEdit, initialStatus, isOpen]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!isOpen) return;\n\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        onClose();\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [isOpen, onClose]);\n\n  if (!isOpen) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!title.trim()) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    onSave(\n      {\n        title: title.trim(),\n        description: description.trim(),\n        status,\n        priority,\n        dueDate,\n      },\n      taskToEdit?.id\n    );\n    onClose();\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"modal-backdrop\" onClick={onClose}>\n      <div\n        className=\"modal-content\"\n        role=\"dialog\"\n        aria-modal=\"true\"\n    >\n            <X size={20} />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"modal-form\">\n          <div className=\"form-group\">\n            <label className=\"form-label\" htmlFor=\"task-title\">\n              タイトル <span style={{ color: 'var(--color-danger)' }}>*</span>\n            </label>\n            <input\n              id=\"task-title\"\n              type=\"text\"\n              className=\"form-input\"\n              value={title}\n              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}\n              placeholder=\"例: 会議資料の作成\"\n              required\n              autoFocus\n            />\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"form-group\">\n            <label className=\"form-label\" <div className=\"form-group\">\n              <label className=\"form-label\" htmlFor=\"task-status\">\n                ステータス\n              </label>\n              <select\n
